A mild prior on the feedback channel (typical positive-feedback rate and negative-to-positive ratio, both readable from any production dashboard without labels) keeps Hierarchical-Informed within 4-13 pp of $Q^\star$ as the bias ratio sweeps from 1:1 to 30:1, with 95% credible intervals covering $Q^\star$ in 50/50 random-seed replicates at $κ_{\max}=10$. Without channel-side priors, every weak-prior variant misses $Q^\star$ by 22-33 pp: the per-cluster sufficient statistics admit a one-parameter family of equally good fits, and the prior on the bias channel (not on latent quality) is what breaks the degeneracy.

</details>


### [180] [Mitigating Context-Memory Conflicts in LLMs through Dynamic Cognitive Reconciliation Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12185)
*Yigeng Zhou,Wu Li,Yifan Lu,Yequan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Wenya Wang,Jun Yu,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段解码方法DCRD，通过分析注意力图预测上下文与记忆之间的冲突，并根据预测结果选择贪心解码或基于上下文保真度的动态解码路径，在四个大语言模型和六个QA数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么在无冲突场景下静态地破坏输出分布，要么难以应对复杂的真实世界情况，缺乏一种既能高效处理知识冲突、又能保持无冲突场景下高准确率和解码效率的动态解码方法。

Method: 提出DCRD两阶段解码方法：第一阶段通过分析注意力图评估上下文保真度并预测潜在冲突；第二阶段根据预测结果将输入导向两条解码路径之一——贪心解码（无冲突）或基于上下文保真度的动态解码（有冲突）。同时构建了知识冲突QA基准ConflictKG。

Result: 在四个LLM和六个QA数据集上的实验表明，DCRD在所有基线方法中表现最优，取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: DCRD能够有效处理上下文-记忆冲突，同时在无冲突场景下保持高准确率和解码效率，验证了注意力图分析在冲突预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models accumulate extensive parametric knowledge through pre-training. However, knowledge conflicts occur when outdated or incorrect parametric knowledge conflicts with external knowledge in the context. Existing methods address knowledge conflicts through contrastive decoding, but in conflict-free scenarios, static approaches disrupt output distribution. Other dynamic decoding methods attempt to measure the degree of conflict but still struggle with complex real-world situations. In this paper, we propose a two-stage decoding method called Dynamic Cognitive Reconciliation Decoding (DCRD), to predict and mitigate context-memory conflicts. DCRD first analyzes the attention map to assess context fidelity and predict potential conflicts. Based on this prediction, the input is directed to one of two decoding paths: (1) greedy decoding, or (2) context fidelity-based dynamic decoding. This design enables DCRD to handle conflicts efficiently while maintaining high accuracy and decoding efficiency in conflict-free cases. Additionally, to simulate scenarios with frequent knowledge updates, we constructed ConflictKG, a knowledge conflict QA benchmark. Experiments on four LLMs across six QA datasets show that DCRD outperforms all baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [181] [Mechanistic Interpretability of ASR models using Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12225)
*Dan Pluth,Zachary Nicholas Houghton,Yu Zhou,Vijay K. Gurbani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将稀疏自编码器（SAE）应用于Transformer-based语音识别模型Whisper，在编码器帧级嵌入上训练高维稀疏潜空间，发现了跨语言和语言边界的多语义特征，并展示了跨语言特征引导的能力，证实了SAE在音频处理模型中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度Transformer NLP模型的内部机制仍不透明，尽管SAE在文本LLM解释性上已证明有效，但尚无研究将其应用于音频处理模型（如ASR），本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将SAE应用于Whisper（基于Transformer的ASR），在编码器的帧级嵌入上训练高维稀疏潜空间。

Result: 发现了跨越语言和非语言边界的多样单语义特征，并实现了跨语言特征引导。

Conclusion: SAE在音频处理模型中是可行的，且Whisper编码了丰富的语言信息。

Abstract: Understanding the internal machinations of deep Transformer-based NLP models is more crucial than ever as these models see widespread use in various domains that affect the public at large, such as industry, academia, finance, health. While these models have advanced rapidly, their internal mechanisms remain largely a mystery. Techniques such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAE) have emerged to understand these mechanisms by projecting dense representations into a sparse vector. While existing research has demonstrated the viability of the SAE in interpreting text-based Large Language Models (LLMs), there are no equivalent studies that demonstrate the application of a SAE to audio processing models like Automatic Speech Recognizers (ASRs). In this work, a SAE is applied to Whisper, a Transformer-based ASR, training a high-dimensional sparse latent space on frame-level embeddings extracted from the Whisper encoder. Our work uncovers diverse monosemantic features across linguistic and non-linguistic boundaries, and demonstrates cross-lingual feature steering. This work establishes the viability of a SAE model and demonstrates that Whisper encodes a rich amount of linguistic information.

</details>


### [182] [Combining On-Policy Optimization and Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12227)
*Miguel Moura Ramos,Duarte M. Alves,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出dGRPO方法，将基于结果的策略优化（GRPO）与基于教师模型的知识蒸馏（OPD）相结合，用于长上下文推理训练，并构建了长上下文合成数据集LongBlocks。实验表明该方法比单独的SFT、KD、GRPO或OPD更稳定有效，能在保持短上下文能力的同时提升长上下文推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文后训练方法存在缺陷：离策略方法（SFT、KD）存在暴露偏差且在长序列中难以从模型自身错误中恢复；在策略RL方法（GRPO）因稀疏奖励而不稳定且样本效率低；在策略蒸馏（OPD）虽提供密集标记级监督但无法直接优化任意奖励信号。现有方法未能有效结合基于结果的策略优化与密集的教师监督。

Method: 提出dGRPO（Distilled Group Relative Policy Optimization），在GRPO框架中融合来自更强教师模型的OPD密集指导，构建包含多跳推理、上下文定位和长文本生成任务的LongBlocks合成数据集，通过单一目标联合优化策略和蒸馏损失。

Result: 实验对比了离策略训练、稀疏奖励GRPO以及dGRPO组合方法，结果表明dGRPO在长上下文推理任务上更稳定有效，同时保留了短上下文能力。

Conclusion: 将基于结果的策略优化与知识蒸馏结合在单一目标中，为长上下文对齐提供了更稳定有效的路径，且不影响短上下文能力。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) to long-context tasks requires post-training methods that remain accurate and coherent over thousands of tokens. Existing approaches are limited in several ways: 1) off-policy methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and knowledge distillation (KD) suffer from exposure bias and limited recovery from model-generated errors over long horizons; 2) on-policy reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) better align training with model-generated states, but are unstable and sample-inefficient due to sparse rewards; 3) on-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level guidance, but does not directly optimize arbitrary reward signals. In this paper, we propose Distilled Group Relative Policy Optimization (dGRPO), a method for long-context reasoning that augments GRPO with dense guidance from a stronger teacher via OPD. We also introduce LongBlocks, a synthetic long-context dataset spanning multi-hop reasoning, contextual grounding, and long-form generation. We conduct extensive experiments and ablations comparing off-policy training, sparse-reward GRPO, and our combined approach, leading to an improved recipe for long-context alignment. Overall, our results show that combining outcome-based policy optimization with knowledge distillation in a single objective provides a more stable and effective path to long-context reasoning, while preserving short-context capabilities.

</details>


### [183] [Mind the Pause: Disfluency-Aware Objective Tuning for Multilingual Speech Correction with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12242)
*Deepak Kumar,Baban Gain,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种多语言ASR转录不流畅校正流水线，结合序列标注器检测不流畅标记与指令微调LLM进行重写，并加入对比学习目标惩罚不流畅标记的生成，在印地语、孟加拉语和马拉地语上取得了优于强基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR转录中常含有填充词、重复、假起始等不流畅现象，降低可读性并影响下游应用（如聊天机器人和语音助手）的可靠性。现有方法多依赖经典模型识别并移除不流畅标记，但会破坏语法结构和语义连贯性；LLM相关研究则主要聚焦于检测或数据增强，缺乏全面的校正方案。

Method: 提出一个多语言校正流水线：(1) 序列标注器（sequence tagger）首先标注不流畅标记；(2) 这些信号用于指导指令微调（instruction fine-tuning）一个LLM将转录重写为流畅文本；(3) 加入对比学习目标（contrastive learning objective），惩罚重写过程中复制不流畅标记的行为，以保留语法和语义。

Result: 在印地语、孟加拉语和马拉地语三种印度语言上的实验表明，该方法一致优于强基线（包括多语言序列到序列模型），证明仅靠检测策略是不够的。

Conclusion: 结合标记级别线索（token-level cues）、指令微调和对比学习，为语音驱动的NLP系统提供了一种实用且可扩展的多语言不流畅校正解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) transcripts often contain disfluencies, such as fillers, repetitions, and false starts, which reduce readability and hinder downstream applications like chatbots and voice assistants. If left unaddressed, such disfluencies can significantly degrade the reliability of downstream systems. Most existing approaches rely on classical models that focus on identifying disfluent tokens for removal. While this strategy is effective to some extent, it often disrupts grammatical structure and semantic coherence, leading to incomplete or unnatural sentences. Recent literature explored the use of large language models (LLMs); however, these efforts have primarily focused on disfluency detection or data augmentation, rather than performing comprehensive correction. We propose a multilingual correction pipeline where a sequence tagger first marks disfluent tokens, and these signals guide instruction fine-tuning of an LLM to rewrite transcripts into fluent text. To further improve reliability, we add a contrastive learning objective that penalizes the reproduction of disfluent tokens, encouraging the model to preserve grammar and meaning while removing disfluent artifacts. Our experiments across three Indian languages, namely Hindi, Bengali, and Marathi show consistent improvements over strong baselines, including multilingual sequence-to-sequence models. These results highlight that detection-only strategies are insufficient. Combining token-level cues with instruction tuning and contrastive learning provides a practical and scalable solution for multilingual disfluency correction in speech-driven NLP systems. We make the codes publicly available at https://github.com/deepak-kumar-98/Mind-the-Pause.

</details>


### [184] [PreScam: A Benchmark for Predicting Scam Progression from Early Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12243)
*Weixiang Sun,Shang Ma,Yiyang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zehong Wang,Colby Nelson,Xusheng Xiao,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PreScam基准，基于177,989份用户报告构建了11,573个多轮对话诈骗实例，涵盖20个诈骗类别，按诈骗杀伤链进行层次化结构标注。实验表明监督编码器在实时终止预测上显著优于零样本LLM，而下一步动作预测对强LLM也仅有中等表现，揭示当前模型在理解诈骗逐步升级方面仍存在明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注静态诈骗检测或合成诈骗，缺乏对真实对话式诈骗随时间演进的建模和理解能力评估，因此需要构建一个能够捕捉诈骗生命周期和多轮心理操纵过程的基准。

Method: 从用户提交的诈骗报告中筛选结构化11,573个对话实例，按提出的诈骗杀伤链进行层次化标注，并在轮次级别标注诈骗者的心理动作和受害者响应。设计了两个任务：实时终止预测和诈骗者动作预测，比较监督编码器和零样本LLM的性能。

Result: 监督编码器在实时终止预测上大幅优于零样本LLM；诈骗者下一步动作预测对强LLM也仅表现中等。模型能捕捉部分诈骗线索，但在追踪风险升级和多轮心理操纵方面仍存在困难。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在表面流畅性与诈骗进程建模之间存在明显差距，虽然能识别一些诈骗相关信号，但难以理解诈骗如何跨轮次逐步升级和操纵受害者，需要更好的诈骗演进建模方法。

Abstract: Conversational scams, such as romance and investment scams, are emerging as a major form of online fraud. Unlike one-shot scam lures such as fake lottery or unpaid toll messages, they unfold through multi-turn conversations in which scammers gradually manipulate victims using evolving psychological techniques. However, existing research mainly focuses on static scam detection or synthetic scams, leaving open whether language models can understand how real-world scams progress over time. We introduce PreScam, a benchmark for modeling scam progression from early conversations. Built from user-submitted scam reports, PreScam filters and structures 177,989 raw reports into 11,573 conversational scam instances spanning 20 scam categories. Each instance is hierarchically structured according to the scam lifecycle defined by the proposed scam kill chain, and further annotated at the turn level with scammer psychological actions and victim responses. We benchmark models on two tasks: real-time termination prediction, which estimates whether a conversation is approaching the termination stage, and scammer action prediction, which forecasts the scammer's subsequent actions. Results show a clear gap between surface-level fluency and progression modeling: supervised encoders substantially outperform zero-shot LLMs on real-time termination prediction, while next-action prediction remains only moderately successful even for strong LLMs. Taken together, these results show that current models can capture some scam-related cues, yet still struggle to track how risk escalates and how manipulation unfolds across turns.

</details>


### [185] [What makes a word hard to learn? Modeling L1 influence on English vocabulary difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12281)
*Jonas Mayer Martins,Zhuojing Huang,Aaricia Herygers,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文使用梯度提升模型，基于熟悉度、语义、表面形式和跨语言迁移等特征，预测以西班牙语、德语或汉语为母语的英语学习者的词汇难度。通过Shapley值分析发现，词汇熟悉度是所有语言群体最重要的特征，但西班牙语和德语学习者还依赖正字法迁移，而汉语学习者则缺乏这种迁移机制，其难度主要由熟悉度和表面特征决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究词汇难度的决定因素，以及这些因素如何随学习者母语的不同而变化，从而为设计个性化词汇教学大纲提供依据。

Method: 使用梯度提升模型（gradient-boosted models），结合熟悉度（如词频）、语义、表面形式和跨语言迁移等特征进行建模，并采用Shapley值计算各特征组的重要性。

Result: 词汇熟悉度是所有三种语言学习者共享的主导特征组。西班牙语和德语学习者的难度预测还依赖于正字法迁移，而汉语学习者由于书写系统差异无法利用这一迁移机制，其难度主要由熟悉度和表面特征共同塑造。

Conclusion: 该模型能够提供可解释的、针对不同母语定制的词汇难度估计，可用于设计词汇教学课程。

Abstract: What makes a word difficult to learn, and how does the difficulty depend on the learner's native language? We computationally model vocabulary difficulty for English learners whose first language is Spanish, German, or Chinese with gradient-boosted models trained on features related to a word's familiarity (e.g., frequency), meaning, surface form, and cross-linguistic transfer. Using Shapley values, we determine the importance of each feature group. Word familiarity is the dominant feature group shared by all three languages. However, predictions for Spanish- and German-speaking learners rely additionally on orthographic transfer. This transfer mechanism is unavailable to Chinese learners, whose difficulty is shaped by a combination of familiarity and surface features alone. Our models provide interpretable, L1-tailored difficulty estimates that can be used to design vocabulary curricula.

</details>


### [186] [TokenRatio: Principled Token-Level Preference Optimization via Ratio Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12288)
*Truong Nguyen,Tien-Phat Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Khoa Doan,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TBPO方法，通过令牌级Bradley-Terry偏好模型和Bregman散度密度比匹配目标，在仅使用序列级成对偏好数据的情况下恢复令牌级偏好最优性，相比基线方法在指令遵循、有用性/无害性和摘要任务上提升了对齐质量和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO虽然广泛用于语言模型对齐，但其建模的是序列级偏好，而生成过程本质上是逐令牌的。现有令牌级扩展方法通常跨时间步分解序列级Bradley-Terry目标，导致每个前缀（状态级）的最优性是隐式的。本文旨在仅使用标准的序列级成对比较数据，恢复令牌级偏好最优性。

Method: 提出TBPO框架：1) 在给定前缀条件下对下一令牌动作建立令牌级Bradley-Terry偏好模型；2) 推导Bregman散度密度比匹配目标，泛化逻辑损失/DPO损失，同时保留令牌级模型诱导的最优策略和DPO的简洁性；3) 提出两个实例化版本——TBPO-Q（显式学习轻量级状态基线）和TBPO-A（通过优势归一化移除基线）。

Result: 在指令遵循、有用性/无害性和摘要基准测试上，TBPO相比强序列级和令牌级基线方法，在以下方面取得改进：对齐质量提升、训练稳定性增强、输出多样性增加。

Conclusion: TBPO成功利用序列级偏好数据恢复了令牌级偏好最优性，通过Bregman散度框架统一了令牌级偏好学习与DPO的简洁性，为语言模型对齐提供了更精细且稳定的训练方法。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used RL-free method for aligning language models from pairwise preferences, but it models preferences over full sequences even though generation is driven by per-token decisions. Existing token-level extensions typically decompose a sequence-level Bradley-Terry objective across timesteps, leaving per-prefix (state-wise) optimality implicit. We study how to recover token-level preference optimality using only standard sequence-level pairwise comparisons. We introduce Token-level Bregman Preference Optimization (TBPO), which posits a token-level Bradley-Terry preference model over next-token actions conditioned on the prefix, and derive a Bregman-divergence density-ratio matching objective that generalizes the logistic/DPO loss while preserving the optimal policy induced by the token-level model and maintaining DPO-like simplicity. We introduce two instantiations: TBPO-Q, which explicitly learns a lightweight state baseline, and TBPO-A, which removes the baseline through advantage normalization. Across instruction following, helpfulness/harmlessness, and summarization benchmarks, TBPO improves alignment quality and training stability and increases output diversity relative to strong sequence-level and token-level baselines.

</details>


### [187] [Overview of the MedHopQA track at BioCreative IX: track description, participation and evaluation of systems for multi-hop medical question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12313)
*Rezarta Islamaj,Joey Chan,Robert Leaman,Jongmyung Jung,Hyeongsoon Hwang,Quoc-An Nguyen,Hoang-Quynh Le,Harikrishnan Gurushankar Saisudha,Ganesh Chandrasekar,Rustam R. Taktashov,Nadezhda Yu. Bizyukova,Sofia I. R. Conceição,Paulo R. C. Lopes,Reem Abdel Salam,Mary Adewunmi,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BioCreative IX MedHopQA共享任务，该任务针对生物医学领域的多跳问答挑战，构建了一个包含1,000个复杂QA对的专用数据集（强调罕见病），并组织了48份提交。结果显示，RAG等检索增强策略显著优于基线LLM，概念级评估（MedCPT）能更准确衡量答案质量。最优系统在MedCPT指标上F1达89.30%，远超零样本基线的67.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学领域多跳问答要求系统整合多个来源的信息来回答复杂问题，这仍然是一个重大挑战。现有基准缺乏针对该领域的专用多跳推理评估数据集，特别是涉及疾病、基因和化学物质跨实体推理的问题。

Method: 构建了1,000个需要两跳推理的QA对（从两个不同维基百科页面整合信息），涵盖疾病、基因和化学物质，重点关注罕见病；采用表面字符串匹配（EM）和概念级准确性（MedCPT分数）两种评估方式；组织48份来自13个团队的提交进行系统评估。

Result: 排名第一的提交在MedCPT指标上F1得分为89.30%，EM得分为87.30%，而零样本基线分别为67.40%和60.20%。RAG及基于检索的策略是取得优异性能的关键。概念级评估能更好地处理表面形式不同但语义正确的答案。

Conclusion: MedHopQA任务表明，检索增强策略对于生物医学多跳问答至关重要，概念级评估比纯字符串匹配更具鲁棒性。该数据集已公开供社区持续研究。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering (QA) remains a significant challenge in the biomedical domain, requiring systems to integrate information across multiple sources to answer complex questions. To address this problem, the BioCreative IX MedHopQA shared task was designed to benchmark in multi-hop reasoning for large language models (LLMs). We developed a novel dataset of 1,000 challenging QA pairs spanning diseases, genes, and chemicals, with particular emphasis on rare diseases. Each question was constructed to require two-hop reasoning through the integration of information from two distinct Wikipedia pages. The challenge attracted 48 submissions from 13 teams. Systems were evaluated using both surface string comparison and conceptual accuracy (MedCPT score). The results showed a substantial performance gap between baseline LLMs and enhanced systems. The top-ranked submission achieved an 89.30% F1 score on the MedCPT metric and an 87.30% exact match (EM) score, compared with 67.40% and 60.20%, respectively, for the zero-shot baseline. A central finding of the challenge was that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and related retrieval-based strategies were critical for strong performance. In addition, concept-level evaluation improved answer assessment when correct responses differed in surface form. The MedHopQA dataset is publicly available to support continued progress in this important area. Challenge materials: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/research/bionlp/medhopqa and benchmark https://www.codabench.org/competitions/7609/

</details>


### [188] [Output Composability of QLoRA PEFT Modules for Plug-and-Play Attribute-Controlled Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12345)
*Michela Lorandi,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种超越单任务训练/推理的泛化方法：联合训练多数据集、推理时组合PEFT模块权重矩阵、推理时组合PEFT模块输出。实验表明，输出求和是一种强大的组合方法，在情感控制任务上平均性能提升2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调(PEFT)技术虽能以较低成本进行任务特定微调，但每个新任务都需要单独微调，缺乏泛化能力。本文旨在探索如何让PEFT超越单任务训练/推理，实现多任务泛化。

Method: 探索三种泛化方法：(i)在多个相关数据集组合上训练；(ii)推理时组合单独训练的PEFT模块的权重矩阵；(iii)推理时组合单独训练的PEFT模块的输出。在三种LLM上使用QLoRA方法，针对情感控制、主题控制和多属性控制三类受控文本生成数据集进行测试。

Result: 求和PEFT模块输出是一种特别强大的组合方法，持续优于或匹配其他方法。即使是与单任务专用模块在单任务测试集上比较，三模块输出组合在情感控制任务上平均性能提升2%。

Conclusion: PEFT模块输出组合（尤其是求和）是一种有效的多任务泛化策略，能够在无需额外训练的情况下提升性能，为PEFT的跨任务泛化提供了简单而强大的解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) techniques offer task-specific fine-tuning at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, but require separate fine-tuning for every new task (combination). In this paper, we explore three ways of generalising beyond single-task training/inference: (i) training on combinations of multiple, related datasets; (ii) at inference, composing the weight matrices of separately trained PEFT modules; and (iii) at inference, composing the outputs of separately trained PEFT modules. We test these approaches on three different LLMs, QLoRA as the PEFT technique, and three sets of controlled text generation datasets for sentiment control, topic control, and multi-attribute control. We find that summing PEFT module outputs is a particularly strong composition method, which consistently either outperforms or matches the performance of alternative approaches. This is the case even when comparing against single-task specialised modules on the single-task test set, where three-module output composition achieves an average 2% point performance increase across all models for sentiment control.

</details>


### [189] [MedHopQA: A Disease-Centered Multi-Hop Reasoning Benchmark and Evaluation Framework for LLM-Based Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12361)
*Rezarta Islamaj,Robert Leaman,Joey Chan,Nicholas Wan,Qiao Jin,Natalie Xie,John Wilbur,Shubo Tian,Lana Yeganova,Po-Ting Lai,Chih-Hsuan Wei,Yifan Yang,Yao Ge,Qingqing Zhu,Zhizheng Wang,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedHopQA是一个以疾病为中心的多跳推理基准，包含1,000个专家策划的问答题对，要求模型整合两个不同维基百科文章的信息，采用开放式自由文本格式，并通过本体同义词集支持词法和概念级评估，旨在解决现有生物医学问答基准中模式匹配与推理区分不足、性能饱和及数据污染等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学问答基准存在以下局限：1）多项选择格式使模型可通过排除法而非推理作答；2）广泛使用的考试风格数据集面临性能饱和和训练数据污染风险；3）多跳推理能力——即整合多源信息进行诊断支持、文献发现和假设生成的核心临床能力——在现有基准中代表性不足。

Method: MedHopQA的构建过程包括：1）1,000个专家策划的问答题对，每个问题需整合两个不同维基百科文章的信息；2）采用开放式自由文本格式而非多项选择；3）结合MONDO、NCBI Gene和NCBI Taxonomy的本体同源集进行注释增强；4）结构化流程涵盖人工标注、分类、迭代验证和LLM-as-a-judge验证；5）为降低排行榜作弊和污染风险，将1,000个评分问题嵌入10,000个问题中，答案保密，通过CodaBench排行榜管理。

Result: MedHopQA作为BioCreative IX的共享任务发布，提供了抗饱和性和抗污染性强的多跳推理基准，可区分模型的真正推理能力与模式匹配能力。

Conclusion: MedHopQA不仅是一个基准数据集，还提供了一个可复用的框架，用于构建以组合推理、抗饱和性和抗污染性为核心设计约束的未来生物医学问答数据集。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in the biomedical domain requires benchmarks that can distinguish reasoning from pattern matching and remain discriminative as model capabilities improve. Existing biomedical question answering (QA) benchmarks are limited in this respect. Multiple-choice formats can allow models to succeed through answer elimination rather than inference, while widely circulated exam-style datasets are increasingly vulnerable to performance saturation and training data contamination. Multi-hop reasoning, defined as the ability to integrate information across multiple sources to derive an answer, is central to clinically meaningful tasks such as diagnostic support, literature-based discovery, and hypothesis generation, yet remains underrepresented in current biomedical QA benchmarks. MedHopQA is a disease-centered multi-hop reasoning benchmark consisting of 1,000 expert-curated question-answer pairs introduced as a shared task at BioCreative IX. Each question requires synthesis of information across two distinct Wikipedia articles, and answers are provided in an open-ended free-text format. Gold annotations are augmented with ontology-grounded synonym sets from MONDO, NCBI Gene, and NCBI Taxonomy to support both lexical and concept-level evaluation. MedHopQA was constructed through a structured process combining human annotation, triage, iterative verification, and LLM-as-a-judge validation. To reduce leaderboard gaming and contamination risk, the 1,000 scored questions are embedded within a publicly downloadable set of 10,000 questions, with answers withheld, on a CodaBench leaderboard. MedHopQA provides both a benchmark and a reusable framework for constructing future biomedical QA datasets that prioritize compositional reasoning, saturation resistance, and contamination resistance as core design constraints.

</details>


### [190] [Context Convergence Improves Answering Inferential Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12370)
*Jamshid Mozafari,Bhawna Piryani,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在开放域推断性问答中的表现，提出了一种基于"收敛性"（convergence）的段落构建方法，即通过句子消除错误答案的能力来组织信息。实验表明，使用高收敛性句子构建的段落比基于余弦相似度的方法显著提升回答准确性，且按收敛性降序排列句子能进一步改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放域问答中广泛应用，但其处理推断性问题（需推导而非直接检索答案）的能力尚未充分探索。现有研究缺乏对段落结构和质量如何影响模型推断性能的系统分析。

Method: 提出"收敛性"（convergence）作为段落构建准则，衡量句子（线索）消除错误答案的有效性。使用TriviaHG数据集子集，组合不同收敛性水平的句子构建段落，评估六种不同规模和架构的大语言模型。

Result: 高收敛性句子构建的段落相比余弦相似度选择方法显著提升答案准确率；按收敛性降序排列句子可轻微改善性能，表明模型倾向于优先利用早期信息丰富的线索。

Conclusion: 收敛性可作为指导段落构建和分析大语言模型推断性推理行为的实用信号。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used in open-domain Question Answering (QA), their ability to handle inferential questions-where answers must be derived rather than directly retrieved-remains still underexplored. This study investigates how the structure and quality of passages influence LLM performance on such questions. We focus on convergence, a measure of how effectively sentences (hints) eliminate incorrect answers, as a criterion for constructing passages. Using subsets of the TriviaHG dataset, we form passages by combining sentences with varying convergence levels and evaluate six LLMs of different sizes and architectures. Our results show that passages built from higher convergence sentences lead to substantially better answer accuracy than those selected by cosine similarity, indicating that convergence captures meaningful relevance for inferential reasoning. Additionally, ordering sentences by descending convergence slightly improves performance, suggesting that LLMs tend to prioritize earlier, information-rich cues. These findings highlight convergence as a practical signal for guiding passage construction and analyzing inferential reasoning behavior in LLMs.

</details>


### [191] [Scalable Token-Level Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12384)
*Rui Min,Tianyu Pang,Chao Du,Minhao Cheng,Yi R. Fung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokenHD提出了一种无需预定义步骤分割的token级幻觉检测框架，通过大规模合成数据引擎和重要性加权训练策略，使小型检测器（0.6B）超越大型推理模型（如QwQ-32B），并展示了从0.6B到8B的规模扩展性和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法在推理密集型任务中面临两个主要问题：一是基于步骤级别的分析粒度不足，二是依赖步骤分割导致可扩展性差。因此需要一种更细粒度、无需预定义分割的检测方案。

Method: TokenHD包含三个核心组件：(1) 可扩展的数据引擎，用于合成大规模token级幻觉标注；(2) 重要性加权训练策略，实现鲁棒的模型训练；(3) 严格的评估协议。检测器直接在自由文本上操作，无需步骤分割或文本重格式化。

Result: 小规模检测器（0.6B）经过TokenHD训练后性能大幅提升，超越大型推理模型QwQ-32B；检测性能随模型规模（0.6B到8B）一致提升；检测器在多样化实际场景中具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: TokenHD证明了token级幻觉检测的有效性和可扩展性，无需依赖步骤分割即可实现高性能检测，且具备跨域泛化能力，为推理密集型任务中的幻觉检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, but they still frequently produce hallucinations. These hallucinations are difficult to detect in reasoning-intensive tasks, where the content appears coherent but contains errors like logical flaws and unreliable intermediate results. While step-level analysis is commonly used to detect internal hallucinations, it suffers from limited granularity and poor scalability due to its reliance on step segmentation. To address these limitations, we propose TokenHD, a holistic pipeline for training token-level hallucination detectors. Specifically, TokenHD consists of a scalable data engine for synthesizing large-scale hallucination annotations along with a training recipe featuring an importance-weighted strategy for robust model training. To systematically assess the detection performance, we also provide a rigorous evaluation protocol. Through training within TokenHD, our detector operates directly on free-form text to identify hallucinations, eliminating the need for predefined step segmentation or additional text reformatting. Our experiments show that even a small detector (0.6B) achieves substantial performance gains after training, surpassing much larger reasoning models (e.g., QwQ-32B), and detection performance scales consistently with model size from 0.6B to 8B. Finally, we show that our detector can generalize well across diverse practical scenarios and explore strategies to further enhance its cross-domain generalization capability.

</details>


### [192] [A Comparative Study of Controlled Text Generation Systems Using Level-Playing-Field Evaluation Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12395)
*Michela Lorandi,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种标准化、公平的对比评估方法（LPF），对多种受控文本生成系统进行重新评估，发现原有性能结果普遍偏高，凸显了标准化评估的迫切性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前受控文本生成（CTG）领域存在评估方法不统一、数据集各异的问题，难以客观比较不同系统的真实性能，亟需一种公平且信息丰富的评估框架。

Method: 采用水平竞技场（LPF）方法：(i) 以标准化方式生成并处理所有系统输出；(ii) 使用当前通用的评估方法与数据集进行统一评估，确保公平比较。

Result: 对代表性CTG系统进行重新评估后，性能结果与原始报告存在显著差异，且多数情况下表现更差。

Conclusion: LPF评估揭示的差异表明CTG领域亟需标准化、可复现的评估实践，否则已发表的性能声明可能严重误导对系统真实能力的判断。

Abstract: Background: Many different approaches to controlled text generation (CTG) have been proposed over recent years, but it is difficult to get a clear picture of which approach performs best, because different datasets and evaluation methods are used in each case to assess the control achieved.
  Objectives: Our aim in the work reported in this paper is to develop an approach to evaluation that enables us to comparatively evaluate different CTG systems in a manner that is both informative and fair to the individual systems.
  Methods: We use a level-playing-field (LPF) approach to comparative evaluation where we (i) generate and process all system outputs in a standardised way, and (ii) apply a shared set of evaluation methods and datasets, selected based on those currently in use, in order to ensure fair evaluation.
  Results: When re-evaluated in this way, performance results for a representative set of current CTG systems differ substantially from originally reported results, in most cases for the worse. This highlights the importance of a shared standardised way of assessing controlled generation.
  Conclusions: The discrepancies revealed by LPF evaluation demonstrate the urgent need for standardised, reproducible evaluation practices in CTG. Our results suggest that without such practices, published performance claims may substantially misrepresent true system capabilities.

</details>


### [193] [Question Difficulty Estimation for Large Language Models via Answer Plausibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12398)
*Jamshid Mozafari,Bhawna Piryani,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-DAPS方法，通过计算候选答案的合理性分数熵值来估计问题难度，在四个QA数据集上优于基线方法，且具有鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问题难度估计方法（可读性公式、检索信号、流行度统计）未能充分捕捉现代LLM面临的推理挑战，需要更有效的难度评估方法。

Method: Q-DAPS通过计算候选答案上的合理性分数分布的熵值来量化问题难度，熵值越高表示答案不确定性越大，问题越难。

Result: 在TriviaQA、NQ、MuSiQue和QASC四个数据集上持续优于基线方法；对超参数变化和问题类型具有强鲁棒性；消融实验表明在不同合理性估计范式、模型规模和现实场景下均保持稳健；与人类判断高度一致。

Conclusion: Q-DAPS为现代QA系统提供了一种可解释、可扩展且抗偏差的问题难度估计方法。

Abstract: Estimating question difficulty is a critical component in evaluating and improving large language models (LLMs) for question answering (QA). Existing approaches often rely on readability formulas, retrieval-based signals, or popularity statistics, which may not fully capture the reasoning challenges posed to modern LLMs. In this paper, we introduce Q-DAPS (Question Difficulty based on Answer Plausibility Scores) method, a novel approach that estimates question difficulty by computing the entropy of plausibility scores over candidate answers. We systematically evaluate Q-DAPS across four prominent QA datasets-TriviaQA, NQ, MuSiQue, and QASC-demonstrating that it consistently outperforms baselines. Moreover, Q-DAPS shows strong robustness across hyperparameter variations and question types. Extensive ablation studies further show that Q-DAPS remains robust across different plausibility estimation paradigms, model sizes, and realistic settings. Human evaluations further confirm strong alignment between Q-DAPS's difficulty estimates and human judgments of question difficulty. Overall, Q-DAPS provides an interpretable, scalable, and bias-resilient approach to question difficulty estimation in modern QA systems.

</details>


### [194] [Stories in Space: In-Context Learning Trajectories in Conceptual Belief Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12412)
*Eric Bigelow,Raphaël Sarfati,Daniel Wurgaft,Owen Lewis,Thomas McGrath,Jack Merullo,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM的上下文学习可视为低维几何空间（概念信念空间）中的信念更新轨迹，通过故事理解任务验证了这一几何框架下的信念动态，并证明线性探针可解码并因果操控这些轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将LLM的上下文学习视为贝叶斯推断，但其所依赖的潜在假设空间结构尚不明确。本文旨在揭示这一空间的内在几何结构。

Method: 以故事理解为动态信念更新的自然场景，结合行为分析（模型输出）与表征分析（内部表示），研究信念更新轨迹；使用线性探针解码行为，并对内部表示进行因果干预。

Result: (1)信念更新可用低维结构化流形上的轨迹描述；(2)该结构在行为与内部表示中一致反映，线性探针可解码预测行为；(3)对表示的因果干预可操控信念轨迹，且效应可从概念空间的几何性质预测。

Conclusion: 为LLM中的信念动态提供了几何解释，将上下文学习的贝叶斯解释扎根于结构化的概念表征中。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) update their behavior in context, which can be viewed as a form of Bayesian inference. However, the structure of the latent hypothesis space over which this inference operates remains unclear. In this work, we propose that LLMs assign beliefs over a low-dimensional geometric space - a conceptual belief space - and that in-context learning corresponds to a trajectory through this space as beliefs are updated over time. Using story understanding as a natural setting for dynamic belief updating, we combine behavioral and representational analyses to study these trajectories. We find that (1) belief updates are well-described as trajectories on low-dimensional, structured manifolds; (2) this structure is reflected consistently in both model behavior and internal representations and can be decoded with simple linear probes to predict behavior; and (3) interventions on these representations causally steer belief trajectories, with effects that can be predicted from the geometry of the conceptual space. Together, our results provide a geometric account of belief dynamics in LLMs, grounding Bayesian interpretations of in-context learning in structured conceptual representations.

</details>


### [195] [ORBIT: Preserving Foundational Language Capabilities in GenRetrieval via Origin-Regulated Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12419)
*Neha Verma,Nikhil Mehta,Shao-Chuan Wang,Naijing Zhang,Alicia Tsai,Li Wei,Lukasz Heldt,Lichan Hong,Ed Chi,Xinyang Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对生成式检索微调中LLM灾难性遗忘问题，提出ORBIT方法，通过跟踪模型权重距离并采用权重平均策略约束参数漂移，在保持检索性能的同时有效保留语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成式检索任务微调时会出现灾难性遗忘，快速丧失通用语言推理能力，且遗忘程度与微调前后参数距离相关。

Method: ORBIT方法：主动追踪微调模型与初始模型权重之间的距离，当该距离超过最大阈值时，采用权重平均策略约束模型漂移。

Result: ORBIT在文本生成和检索性能上均优于通用持续学习基线及相关正则化方法。

Conclusion: 通过约束模型参数漂移可以有效缓解生成式检索微调中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancements in large language model (LLM) development, fine-tuning them for specific tasks often results in the catastrophic forgetting of their general, language-based reasoning abilities. This work investigates and addresses this challenge in the context of the Generative Retrieval (GenRetrieval) task. During GenRetrieval fine-tuning, we find this forgetting occurs rapidly and correlates with the distance between the fine-tuned and original model parameters. Given these observations, we propose ORBIT, a novel approach that actively tracks the distance between fine-tuned and initial model weights, and uses a weight averaging strategy to constrain model drift during GenRetrieval fine-tuning when this inter-model distance exceeds a maximum threshold. Our results show that ORBIT retains substantial text and retrieval performance by outperforming both common continual learning baselines and related regularization methods that also employ weight averaging.

</details>


### [196] [Predicting Disagreement with Human Raters in LLM-as-a-Judge Difficulty Assessment without Using Generation-Time Probability Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12422)
*Yo Ehara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需生成时概率信号、利用嵌入空间几何一致性来预测LLM生成难度评级中哪些可能与人评分不一致的方法，实验证明该方法优于基于概率的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自动生成教育材料难度评级虽广泛应用，但人机之间评分不一致问题严重；现有依赖生成时概率信号的方法难以跨模型比较，且需在生成时收集，效率低下。

Method: 利用难度评级的序数性质，使用ModernBERT等嵌入空间，基于评级集合的几何一致性来识别可能与人评分不一致的候选样本，无需生成时概率信号。

Result: 在GPT-OSS-120B和Qwen3-235B-A22B两个模型上的英语CEFR句子难度评估实验中，所提方法在预测与人评分不一致方面的AUC高于基于概率的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了LLM难度评级与人评分一致性的预测能力，可实现对不一致样本的针对性重评，减少人工成本。

Abstract: Automatic generation of educational materials using large language models (LLMs) is becoming increasingly common, but assigning difficulty levels to such materials still requires substantial human effort. LLM-as-a-Judge has therefore attracted attention, yet disagreement with human raters remains a major challenge. We propose a method for predicting which LLM-generated difficulty ratings are likely to disagree with human raters, so that such cases can be sent for re-rating. Unlike prior approaches, our method does not rely on generation-time probability signals, which must be collected during rating generation and are often difficult to compare across LLMs. Instead, exploiting the fact that difficulty is an ordinal scale, we use a separate embedding space, such as ModernBERT, and identify disagreement candidates based on the geometric consistency of the rating set. Experiments on English CEFR-based sentence difficulty assessment with GPT-OSS-120B and Qwen3-235B-A22B showed that the proposed method achieved higher AUC for predicting disagreement with human raters than probability-based baselines.

</details>


### [197] [Geometric Factual Recall in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12426)
*Shauli Ravfogel,Gilad Yehudai,Joan Bruna,Alberto Bietti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了一种不同于传统"键值记忆"的几何形式事实记忆机制：在单层Transformer中，主体嵌入以线性叠加的方式编码多个属性，MLP充当关系条件选择器而非关联记忆，只需对数级别的嵌入维度即可记忆随机双射。该机制在多跳推理中展现出容量-深度权衡，且MLP学到的选择机制可零样本迁移到全新事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战Transformer语言模型将权重矩阵视为"关联记忆"（参数数量随事实数量线性增长）的传统观点，探索一种更高效、更具几何意义的记忆机制。

Method: 在单层Transformer需记忆随机双射（主体到共享属性集）的受控设定下，通过理论证明给出构造性方案：主体嵌入编码属性向量的线性叠加，小型MLP通过ReLU门控机制作为关系条件选择器提取属性。进一步将结果推广到多跳推理场景，给出有无思维链的构造，并推导信息论下界。

Result: 证明对数级别的嵌入维度足以记忆随机双射；多跳推理中存在容量-深度权衡；梯度下降可自动发现具有预测结构的解；训练后的MLP在主体嵌入重新初始化后能零样本迁移到全新双射。

Conclusion: Transformer的记忆机制本质上可能是几何的而非关联的，MLP学习到的是通用的选择机制而非特定事实，这为理解语言模型的知识存储与泛化提供了新的视角。

Abstract: How do transformer language models memorize factual associations? A common view casts internal weight matrices as associative memories over pairs of embeddings, requiring parameter counts that scale linearly with the number of facts. We develop a theoretical and empirical account of an alternative, \emph{geometric} form of memorization in which learned embeddings encode relational structure directly, and the MLP plays a qualitatively different role. In a controlled setting where a single-layer transformer must memorize random bijections from subjects to a shared attribute set, we prove that a logarithmic embedding dimension suffices: subject embeddings encode \emph{linear superpositions} of their associated attribute vectors, and a small MLP acts as a relation-conditioned selector that extracts the relevant attribute via ReLU gating, and not as an associative key-value mapping. We extend these results to the multi-hop setting -- chains of relational queries such as ``Who is the mother of the wife of $x$?'' -- providing constructions with and without chain-of-thought that exhibit a provable capacity-depth tradeoff, complemented by a matching information-theoretic lower bound. Empirically, gradient descent discovers solutions with precisely the predicted structure. Once trained, the MLP transfers zero-shot to entirely new bijections when subject embeddings are appropriately re-initialized, revealing that it has learned a generic selection mechanism rather than memorized any particular set of facts.

</details>


### [198] [A Causal Language Modeling Detour Improves Encoder Continued Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12438)
*Rian Touchent,Eric de la Clergerie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种“CLM迂回”策略：在领域自适应中先用因果语言模型（CLM）训练，再短时间用掩码语言模型（MLM）微调，在生物医学文本上显著提升下游任务性能。实验证明CLM的密集监督主要影响低层Transformer层，且这种表征变化在后续MLM阶段得以保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的领域自适应方法通常直接使用MLM继续训练编码器。作者希望探索是否可以通过临时切换为CLM训练来获得更好的下游性能，并理解其背后的原因。

Method: 在领域自适应过程中，先使用CLM训练编码器，然后切换回MLM进行短时间衰减训练（decay）。在ModernBERT架构上，对法语和英语生物医学文本进行实验，并在8个法语和11个英语生物医学下游任务上评估。

Result: CLM迂回策略在法语任务上提升1.2-2.8个百分点，英语任务上提升0.3-0.8个百分点。CLM的密集监督主要影响低层Transformer层（0-7层），冻结低层会消除收益，而冻结中层则保留收益。表征变化在后续MLM衰减阶段得以保持。

Conclusion: CLM迂回是一种简单有效的领域自适应改进策略，其收益源于CLM对低层Transformer层的更强监督。研究同时发布了两款SOTA生物医学编码器模型。

Abstract: When adapting an encoder to a new domain, the standard approach is to continue training with Masked Language Modeling (MLM). We show that temporarily switching to Causal Language Modeling (CLM) followed by a short MLM decay improves downstream performance. On biomedical texts with ModernBERT, this CLM detour outperforms MLM baselines trained on identical data and compute across 8 French and 11 English biomedical tasks, by +1.2-2.8pp and +0.3-0.8pp respectively, depending on model size. We investigate the reasons for these gains. We find that CLM's dense supervision impacts low transformer layers (0-7) far more than MLM does. Freezing low layers during CLM eliminates the downstream benefit; freezing mid layers preserves it. The representational changes persist through the MLM decay phase, even when it matches the CLM phase in length, and they scale with model capacity. We release ModernCamemBERT-bio and ModernBERT-bio as state-of-the-art biomedical encoders in Base and Large sizes.

</details>


### [199] [The Algorithmic Caricature: Auditing LLM-Generated Political Discourse Across Crisis Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12452)
*Gunjan,Sidahmed Benabderrahmane,Talal Rahwan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出从计算社会科学视角检测合成政治话语，通过对比9个危机事件中178万条真实与合成帖子的情感强度、结构规律性、词汇意识形态框架和跨事件依赖性四个维度，发现合成话语虽流畅但群体层面不真实，表现为更消极、更规律、更抽象，差异程度取决于事件类型，并总结为"漫画差距"指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测多聚焦句子级线索（困惑度、突发性、token异常），但随着生成模型改进这些信号可能减弱；需要从群体层面评估合成政治话语的社会真实性。

Method: 构建涵盖9个危机事件的1,789,406条配对语料库，从情感强度、结构规律性、词汇-意识形态框架、跨事件依赖性四个维度，使用均值差距和离散度证据进行对比分析。

Result: 合成话语群体层面不真实：情绪更消极且离散度更低、结构更规律、词汇更抽象。差异对快速变化的去中心化危机更大，对正式或制度中介事件更小。总结为"漫画差距"指标。

Conclusion: 合成政治话语的主要局限不在语法或流畅度，而在于群体现实主义降低。群体层面审计补充了传统文本检测，为评估生成话语的社会现实主义提供了CSS框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate fluent political text at scale, raising concerns about synthetic discourse during crises and social conflict. Existing AI-text detection often focuses on sentence-level cues such as perplexity, burstiness, or token irregularities, but these signals may weaken as generative systems improve. We instead adopt a Computational Social Science perspective and ask whether synthetic political discourse behaves like an observed online population. We construct a paired corpus of 1,789,406 posts across nine crisis events: COVID-19, the Jan. 6 Capitol attack, the 2020 and 2024 U.S. elections, Dobbs/Roe v. Wade, the 2020 BLM protests, U.S. midterms, the Utah shooting, and the U.S.-Iran war. For each event, we compare observed discourse from social platforms with synthetic discourse generated for the same context. We evaluate four dimensions: emotional intensity, structural regularity, lexical-ideological framing, and cross-event dependency, using mean gaps and dispersion evidence. Across events, synthetic discourse is fluent but population-level unrealistic. It is generally more negative and less dispersed in sentiment, structurally more regular, and lexically more abstract than observed discourse. Observed discourse instead shows broader emotional variation, longer-tailed structural distributions, and more context-specific, colloquial lexical markers. These differences are event-dependent: larger for fast-moving, decentralized crises and smaller for formal or institutionally mediated events. We summarize them with a simple event-level measure, the Caricature Gap. Our findings suggest that the main limitation of synthetic political discourse is not grammar or fluency, but reduced population realism. Population-level auditing complements traditional text-detection and provides a CSS framework for evaluating the social realism of generated discourse.

</details>


### [200] [Task-Adaptive Embedding Refinement via Test-time LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12487)
*Ariel Gera,Shir Ashury-Tahan,Gal Bloch,Ohad Eytan,Assaf Toledo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM引导的查询精炼范式，通过在大语言模型反馈下优化查询的嵌入表示，使得嵌入模型能够在零样本搜索和分类任务中实现自适应，在多个基准上取得最高+25%的相对提升，并显著改善排序质量和类别分离度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展嵌入模型在具有挑战性的零样本搜索和分类任务中的可用性，使其在无法大规模部署昂贵LLM管道的实际场景中也能有效发挥作用。

Method: 利用生成式大语言模型在一小部分文档上的反馈，对用户查询的嵌入表示进行实时精炼，从而使嵌入空间能够适应特定任务约束。

Result: 在文献搜索、意图检测、关键点匹配和细粒度指令跟随等任务上取得最高+25%的相对改进，所有模型和数据集上均获得一致性增益，排序质量和二元分离度均有提升。

Conclusion: LLM引导的查询精炼能够有效扩展嵌入模型的部署场景，使其成为在语料库规模下不可行的高成本LLM管道的具有竞争力的替代方案。

Abstract: We explore the effectiveness of an LLM-guided query refinement paradigm for extending the usability of embedding models to challenging zero-shot search and classification tasks. Our approach refines the embedding representation of a user query using feedback from a generative LLM on a small set of documents, enabling embeddings to adapt in real time to the target task. We conduct extensive experiments with state-of-the-art text embedding models across a diverse set of challenging search and classification benchmarks. Empirical results indicate that LLM-guided query refinement yields consistent gains across all models and datasets, with relative improvements of up to +25% in literature search, intent detection, key-point matching, and nuanced query-instruction following. The refined queries improve ranking quality and induce clearer binary separation across the corpus, enabling the embedding space to better reflect the nuanced, task-specific constraints of each ad-hoc user query. Importantly, this expands the range of practical settings in which embedding models can be effectively deployed, making them a compelling alternative when costly LLM pipelines are not viable at corpus-scale. We release our experimental code for reproducibility, at https://github.com/IBM/task-aware-embedding-refinement.

</details>


### [201] [MultiSoc-4D: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Instruction-Induced Label Collapse in Closed-Set LLM Annotation of Bengali Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06940)
*Souvik Pramanik,S. M. Riaz Rahman Antu,Shak Mohammad Abyad,Md. Ibrahim Khalil,Md. Shahriar Hussain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSoc-4D，一个包含58K+条孟加拉语社交媒体评论的四维度（类别、情感、仇恨言论、讽刺）基准数据集。通过让ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude和Grok分别标注数据，系统性地诊断LLM行为，发现了一种普遍存在的现象——"指令诱导的标签坍缩"，即LLM倾向于选择回退标签（Other、Neutral、No），导致对少数类别的漏检（如仇恨言论漏检79%、讽刺漏检75%），并证明这是一种"标签一致幻觉"（讽刺检测的Fleiss' Kappa ≈ -0.001）。在40+个LLM上验证了该标注偏见的传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在低资源语言中对封闭集指令的行为尚未得到充分研究，现有自动标注方法可能存在系统性偏见，需要构建诊断基准来量化与理解这类偏见。

Method: 构建MultiSoc-4D基准数据集（58K+条孟加拉语评论，六来源、四维度标注）；设计结构化标注流水线（ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude、Grok各标注独立分区，共享20%验证集）；通过统计分析（Fleiss' Kappa等）系统诊断LLM标注行为。

Result: 发现"指令诱导的标签坍缩"现象：LLM系统性偏好回退标签；对仇恨言论和讽刺的漏检率分别高达79%和75%；讽刺检测的Fleiss' Kappa ≈ -0.001，验证了"标签一致幻觉"；该偏见在40+个LLM中普遍存在且不受架构差异影响。

Conclusion: LLM在低资源语言标注中存在系统性标签坍缩偏见，导致高一致率掩盖了少数类别的严重漏检；MultiSoc-4D可作为孟加拉语NLP中标注偏见的诊断基准。

Abstract: Annotation automation via Large Language Models (LLMs) is the core approach for scaling NLP datasets; however, LLM behavior with respect to closed-set instructions in low-resource languages has not been well studied. We present MultiSoc-4D, a Bengali social media dataset benchmark, which contains 58K+ social media comments from six sources annotated along four dimensions: category, sentiment, hate speech, and sarcasm. By employing a structured pipeline where ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, and Grok individually annotate separate partitions, while sharing a common validation set of 20%, we diagnose LLM behavior systematically. We discover a prevalent phenomenon called "instruction-induced label collapse", wherein LLMs show a systematic preference towards fallback labels (Other, Neutral, No), leading to high agreement rates but under-detection of minority categories. For example, we find that LLMs failed to detect 79% and 75% of instances with hateful and sarcastic content compared to a human-calibrated reference. Furthermore, we prove that it represents a "label agreement illusion", statistically validated via almost null Fleiss' Kappa ($κ\approx -0.001$) on sarcasm detection. Across 40+ LLMs, we benchmark this annotation bias propagation within the training pipeline, regardless of architectural differences. We release MultiSoc-4D as a diagnostic benchmark for annotation biases in Bengali NLP.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [A Cascaded Generative Approach for e-Commerce Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11118)
*Moein Hasani,Hamidreza Shahidi,Trace Levinson,Yuan Zhong,Guanghua Shu,Vinesh Gudla,Tejaswi Tenneti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种级联商品陈列框架，将电商个性化店铺首页的构建分解为两个生成任务：基于位置的主题生成和约束性关键词生成，结合教师-学生微调提升效率，并与传统排序模型融合，在线实验显示加购率提升2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的电商个性化店铺首页由独立组件（静态主题、检索系统、排序模型）拼装而成，这种方式灵活性差，难以实现页面级别的语义一致性和动态目标优化，也无法适应随时间变化的营销需求。

Method: 提出了一个级联商品陈列框架：① 位置级别的主题生成；② 每个位置上的约束关键词生成以驱动产品检索。采用教师-学生微调技术提升生产效率，并引入AI驱动的内容评估与质量过滤框架实现安全自动化部署，最终将生成式输出与传统点级排序模型融合构建混合基础设施。

Result: 微调模型消融实验性能接近封闭权重的大语言模型。在线实验中，该框架相比强基线在每次页面浏览的加购量上提升约+2.7%。

Conclusion: 级联生成式框架能够有效突破传统刚性架构的局限，在保持混合基础设施的同时实现更好的个性化与语义连贯性，并带来可观的业务指标提升。

Abstract: Personalized storefronts in large e-commerce marketplaces are often assembled from many independent components: static themes per page section ("placement"), retrieval systems to fetch eligible products per placement, and pointwise rankers to order content. While effective in optimizing for aggregate preferences, this paradigm is rigid and can limit personalization and semantic cohesion across the page. This makes it poorly suited to support dynamic objectives and merchandising requirements over time. To address this, we introduce a cascaded merchandising framework that decomposes storefront construction into two generative tasks: (i) placement-level theme generation and (ii) constrained keyword generation per placement to power product retrieval. Teacher-student fine-tuning is leveraged to improve scalability of this framework under production latency and cost constraints. Fine-tuned model ablations are shown to approach closed-weight LLM performance. We further contribute frameworks for AI-driven content evaluation and quality filtering, enabling safe and automated deployment of dynamic content at scale. Generative output is fused with traditional ranking models to preserve hybrid infrastructure. In online experiments, this framework yields an estimated +2.7% lift in cart adds per page view over a strong baseline.

</details>


### [203] [RankQ: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning via Self-Supervised Action Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11151)
*Andrew Choi,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RankQ，一种离线到在线强化学习目标函数，通过自监督多术语排名损失替代传统悲观惩罚，学习结构化动作排序，从而在稀疏奖励D4RL基准、视觉机器人学习和sim-to-real迁移中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线强化学习中，现有悲观方法通过下采样OOD动作来抑制价值高估，但这本质上相当于行为克隆锚点，当数据集动作次优时会阻碍后续在线策略改进。需要一种能学习相对动作偏好而非统一惩罚未见动作的方法。

Method: 提出RankQ，在时序差分学习中引入自监督多术语排名损失，强制学习结构化动作排序。通过学习相对动作偏好来塑造Q函数，使动作梯度指向更高质量的行为，而非统一惩罚未见动作。

Result: 在稀疏奖励D4RL基准上与七种方法竞争或更优；在视觉机器人学习中，低数据 regime 下仿真成功率平均比次优方法高42.7%，高数据 regime 下高13.7%；sim-to-real迁移将真实世界叠方块成功率从43.1%提升至84.7%。

Conclusion: RankQ通过结构化动作排序有效解决了离线到在线强化学习中悲观惩罚限制策略改进的问题，在多种场景下显著提升性能，尤其适用于预训练VLA模型的微调。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) improves sample efficiency by leveraging pre-collected datasets prior to online interaction. A key challenge, however, is learning an accurate critic in large state--action spaces with limited dataset coverage. To mitigate harmful updates from value overestimation, prior methods impose pessimism by down-weighting out-of-distribution (OOD) actions relative to dataset actions. While effective, this essentially acts as a behavior cloning anchor and can hinder downstream online policy improvement when dataset actions are suboptimal. We propose RankQ, an offline-to-online Q-learning objective that augments temporal-difference learning with a self-supervised multi-term ranking loss to enforce structured action ordering. By learning relative action preferences rather than uniformly penalizing unseen actions, RankQ shapes the Q-function such that action gradients are directed toward higher-quality behaviors. Across sparse reward D4RL benchmarks, RankQ achieves performance competitive with or superior to seven prior methods. In vision-based robot learning, RankQ enables effective offline-to-online fine-tuning of a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model in a low-data regime, achieving on average a 42.7% higher simulation success rate than the next best method. In a high-data setting, RankQ improves simulation performance by 13.7% over the next best method and achieves strong sim-to-real transfer, increasing real-world cube stacking success from 43.1% to 84.7% relative to the VLA's initial performance.

</details>


### [204] [The Many Faces of On-Policy Distillation: Pitfalls, Mechanisms, and Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11182)
*Siqi Zhu,Xuyan Ye,Hongyu Lu,Weiye Shi,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在线策略蒸馏(OPD)和在线策略自蒸馏(OPSD)在LLM后训练中的生效条件与失效机理，发现OPD对教师选择和损失函数高度敏感，OPSD在实例特定特权信息缺失时失败，并识别出三种核心失效机制：分布不匹配、优化不稳定和特权信息聚合不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于OPD和OPSD有效性的研究结果相互矛盾——有些显示出优越性，另一些则报告不稳定性和性能退化。本文旨在系统性地解答这些方法何时有效、何时失效及其原因，以指导实际应用。

Method: 对OPD和OPSD在数学推理、系统提示内化、对齐偏好等不同场景下进行大规模实证研究，分析教师选择、损失函数形式、特权信息类型等因素的影响，并识别失效机制，提出缓解方案。

Result: (1) OPD在数学推理中高度依赖教师质量和损失函数设计；(2) OPSD在实例特定特权信息缺失时完全失效，但在共享潜在规则（如系统提示）时有效；(3) 识别出三种失效机制：分布不匹配、TopK反向KL梯度偏差、PI-free策略聚合不足；(4) 停止梯度TopK目标、RLVR适配教师、SFT稳定学生等方法可缓解失效。

Conclusion: OPD和OPSD的有效性高度依赖于应用场景和具体实现细节，选择合适的教师、损失函数和训练策略至关重要；通过针对性缓解措施可以显著改善其稳定性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) have emerged as promising post-training methods for large language models, offering dense token-level supervision on trajectories sampled from the model's own policy. However, existing results on their effectiveness remain mixed: while OP(S)D has shown promise in system prompt and knowledge internalization, recent studies also report instability and degradation. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical study of when OPD and OPSD work, when they fail, and why. We find that OPD on mathematical reasoning is highly sensitive to teacher choice and loss formulation, whereas OPSD fails in our tested settings due to test-time absence of instance-specific privileged information (PI). In contrast, OPSD is effective when PI represents a shared latent rule, such as a system prompt or alignment preference. We identify three failure mechanisms: (1) distribution mismatch between teacher and student caused by conditioning on student-generated prefixes, (2) optimization instability from biased TopK reverse-KL gradients, and (3) an OPSD-specific limitation where the student learns a PI-free policy that aggregates PI-conditioned teachers, which is insufficient when PI is instance-specific. We further show that stop-gradient TopK objectives, RLVR-adapted teachers, and SFT-stabilized students mitigate these failures.

</details>


### [205] [Don't Look at the Numbers: Visual Anchoring Bias and Layer-wise Representation in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11218)
*M. Shalankin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 图像上的嵌入数字锚点会系统性偏置视觉-语言模型的质量判断，锚点效应比严重图像质量退化大2.5倍，且通过分层探测发现锚点分类饱和层与质量预测最优层存在分离，融合分析揭示了架构依赖的集成模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉-语言模型（VLM）在图像质量判断中是否受到嵌入数字锚点（embedded numeric anchors）的系统性偏差影响，并揭示这种偏差的因果机制及其与表征动态的关系。

Method: 在六个来自五个架构家族的VLM上进行实验；使用ANOVA评估锚点效应大小（eta^2=0.18-0.77）；通过分层探测（layer-wise probing）分析锚点分类饱和层与质量预测最优层的关系；采用融合分析（fusion analysis）识别架构依赖的集成模式。

Result: 锚点效应显著且比严重图像质量退化大2.5倍；锚点分类饱和层（L12-L34）对质量预测非最优，最优层更深（R^2=0.69-0.91）；不同模型存在不同的融合模式——两个模型在L1-L2即时融合，三个模型部分融合或不融合。

Conclusion: 建立了视觉锚定偏差的因果解释，将行为易感性（behavioral susceptibility）与表征动态（representation dynamics）联系起来。

Abstract: Embedded numeric anchors on images systematically bias Vision-Language Model quality judgments across six VLMs from five architectural families (ANOVA eta^2 = 0.18-0.77, all p < 0.001). Anchor effects are 2.5x larger than severe image quality degradation, confirming bias is not reducible to visual changes. Layer-wise probing reveals consistent dissociation: layers where anchor classification saturates (L12-L34) are suboptimal for quality prediction, with optimal layers deeper (R^2 = 0.69-0.91). Fusion analysis identifies architecture-dependent integration -- instant fusion at L1-L2 in two models versus partial or no fusion in three others. These results establish a causal account of visual anchoring bias, linking behavioral susceptibility to representation dynamics.

</details>


### [206] [Do Vision-Language-Models show human-like logical problem-solving capability in point and click puzzle games?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11223)
*Dominik Helfenstein,Marco Menner,Maximilian Triebel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VLATIM基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在《不可思议的机器2》物理谜题游戏中的类人逻辑推理能力。实验发现，大型专有模型在规划方面表现优越，但在精确视觉定位和执行方面存在显著不足，尚未达到人类水平的问题解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常忽视了点击解谜游戏中所需的复杂物理推理能力，特别是在高层逻辑推理与连续动作空间（需要精确鼠标交互）之间存在关键缺口。

Method: 构建了基于经典物理谜题游戏《不可思议的机器2》的VLATIM基准，包含五个递进部分，评估从基础视觉定位、领域理解到多步操作和完整谜题求解的能力。

Result: 大型专有模型展现出优越的规划能力，但在精确视觉定位上存在困难，推理与执行之间存在显著差距。模型尚未展现出类人问题解决能力。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在物理谜题推理任务中，高层逻辑规划与底层精确执行之间存在显著脱节，尚未达到人类水平的综合问题解决能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language(-Action) Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to interactive environments, yet existing benchmarks often overlook the complex physical reasoning required for point-and-click puzzle games. This paper introduces Vision-Language Against The Incredible Machine (VLATIM), a benchmark designed to evaluate human-like logical problem-solving capabilities within the classic physics puzzle game The Incredible Machine 2 (TIM). Unlike existing benchmarks, VLATIM specifically targets the critical gap between high-level logical reasoning and continuous action spaces requiring precise mouse interactions. This benchmark is structured into five progressive parts, assessing capabilities that range from basic visual grounding and domain understanding to multi-step manipulation and full puzzle solving. Our results reveal a significant disparity between reasoning and execution. While large proprietary models demonstrate superior planning abilities, they struggle with precise visual grounding. Consequently, they do not yet show human-like problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [207] [PIVOT: Bridging Planning and Execution in LLM Agents via Trajectory Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11225)
*Tuo Zhang,Alin-Ionut Popa,Yan Xu,Rui Song,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PIVOT是一个通过自监督轨迹优化框架解决LLM智能体计划与执行之间不一致性的方法，通过PLAN-INSPECT-EVOLVE-VERIFY四个阶段迭代改进轨迹，在DeepPlanning和GAIA基准测试上实现了SOTA性能，完全自主变体仍保持显著提升，且计算效率比竞争方法高3-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体经常生成看似合理的计划但在执行时失败，原因包括不可行的行动、约束违反以及长期执行中的累积错误，亟需解决计划与执行之间的对齐问题。

Method: PIVOT框架包含四个阶段：PLAN（生成候选轨迹）、INSPECT（执行轨迹并计算结构化损失及文本梯度）、EVOLVE（利用这些信号生成改进轨迹）、VERIFY（最终全局检查任务约束）。采用单调接受过程确保解质量非递减。

Result: 在DeepPlanning和GAIA上达到SOTA性能：人工反馈版本在约束满足上实现高达94%的相对改进；完全自主变体仍保持显著增益；计算效率上比竞争方法节省3-5倍token消耗。

Conclusion: 基于（自我或人工监督的）反馈的轨迹优化是缓解自主智能体系统中计划-执行鸿沟的一种原则性方法论。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents frequently generate seemingly coherent plans that fail upon execution due to infeasible actions, constraint violations, and compounding errors over extended horizons. PIVOT (Plan-Inspect-eVOlve Trajectories) addresses this plan-execution misalignment through a self-supervised framework that treats trajectories as optimizable objects iteratively refined via environment interaction. The framework comprises four stages: PLAN generates candidate trajectories; INSPECT executes them and computes structured losses with textual gradients encoding plan-execution discrepancies; EVOLVE applies these signals to produce improved trajectories; and VERIFY performs a final global check against task constraints. A monotonic acceptance process ensures a non-decreasing solution quality. Empirical evaluations on DeepPlanning and GAIA demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: with human-in-the-loop (HITL) feedback, PIVOT establishes a strong upper bound up to 94% relative improvement in constraint satisfaction, while its fully autonomous variant retains substantial gains, showing that the core trajectory-refinement mechanism remains effective without external supervision. At the same time, PIVOT remains computationally efficient, requiring up to 3x to 5x fewer tokens than competing refinement methods. These findings establish that (self- or human-supervised) feedback-based trajectory optimization is a principled methodology for mitigating plan-execution gaps in autonomous agent systems.

</details>


### [208] [Unlocking LLM Creativity in Science through Analogical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11258)
*Andrew Shen,Shaul Druckmann,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出类比推理（AR）方法，通过跨领域类比生成多样化解决方案，缓解LLM在开放式问题中的模式坍缩问题，在4个生物医学任务上实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科学需要AI系统在开放式问题中持续生成新颖且多样化的解决方案，但现有LLM容易产生模式坍缩（低多样性生成），限制了其在生物医学等复杂领域的应用。

Method: 提出类比推理（AR）方法：首先基于共享关系结构生成跨领域问题的类比，然后利用这些类比搜索新颖解决方案，从而提升生成结果的多样性。

Result: AR相比基线将多样性指标提升90-173%；超过50%概率生成全新方案（基线最低仅1.6%）；在扰动效应预测中分布指标提升近13倍；细胞通讯预测AUPRC优于所有基线；脑区交互推断Spearman相关系数ρ=0.729；寡核苷酸性质预测在两个数据集上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: AR方法能有效增强现有方案生成方法的搜索空间，为自主科学中的开放式问题求解提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous science promises to augment scientific discovery, particularly in complex fields like biomedicine. However, this requires AI systems that can consistently generate novel and diverse solutions to open-ended problems. We evaluate LLMs on the task of open-ended solution generation and quantify their tendency to mode collapse into low-diversity generations. To mitigate this mode collapse, we introduce analogical reasoning (AR) as a new approach to solution generation. AR generates analogies to cross-domain problems based on shared relational structure, then uses those analogies to search for novel solutions. Compared to baselines, AR discovers significantly more diverse generations (improving solution diversity metrics by 90-173%), generates novel solutions over 50% of the time (compared to as little as 1.6% for baselines), and produces high-quality analogies. To validate the real-world feasibility of AR, we implement AR-generated solutions across four biomedical problems, yielding consistent quantitative gains. AR-generated approaches achieve a nearly 13-fold improvement on distributional metrics for perturbation effect prediction, outperform all baselines on AUPRC when predicting cell-cell communication, infer brain region interactions with a high Spearman correlation ($ρ$=0.729) to published methods, and establish state-of-the-art performance on 2 datasets for oligonucleotide property prediction. The novel and diverse solutions produced by AR can be used to augment the search space of existing solution generation methods.

</details>


### [209] [Template-as-Ontology: Configurable Synthetic Data Infrastructure for Cross-Domain Manufacturing AI Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11259)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"模板即本体"原则，通过单一Python配置模块同时定义制造仿真器规范和AI工具运行时领域模式，构建了从仿真到分析的五层管道，在66个实体类型上生成符合ISA-95标准的MES数据。实验验证了参数可控性，并通过本体约束将AI工具参数幻觉率从43%降至0%（架构性保证，不依赖模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的AI智能体部署在制造环境时需要符合模式的填充数据进行验证，但生产MES数据具有专有性、隐私受限且供应商特定的特点，缺乏可用的验证数据层。

Method: 提出Template-as-Ontology原则，将单一Python配置模块同时作为时步制造仿真器的规范和AI分析工具的运行时领域模式；构建五层管道（仿真→PostgreSQL→CDC/Iceberg湖仓→星型模式→12个参数化AI工具），通过单源消费保证结构对齐。

Result: 6个行业模板（航空航天、制药、汽车、电子、饮料、仓储）在相同框架代码上运行；60次校准实验确认参数可控；72次工具调用的幻觉实验中，本体约束将参数幻觉率从43%降至0%（Fisher精确检验p<10^-12），且0%为架构性保证，不依赖模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为离散制造AI验证提供了可复用的数据层，通过架构设计而非集成手段实现仿真与分析层的对齐，从根本上消除了工具参数捏造问题。

Abstract: LLarge language model (LLM)-based AI agents deployed in manufacturing environments require populated, schema-correct data for validation, yet production MES data is proprietary, privacy-encumbered, and vendor-specific. This paper introduces the Template-as-Ontology principle: a single Python configuration module (700-770 lines, 45 validated exports) serves simultaneously as the specification for a time-stepped manufacturing simulator and as the runtime domain schema for AI analytics tools, producing alignment by construction rather than integration. We formally define the domain template as a typed relational configuration schema and prove that structural alignment between simulation and tool layers is guaranteed by single-source consumption. A five-layer pipeline--simulation, PostgreSQL, CDC/Iceberg lakehouse, star schema, and 12 parameterized AI tools--generates causally coherent, MES-shaped data spanning 66 entity types across four operational domains mapped to ISA-95/IEC 62264. We validate the architecture with six industry templates (aerospace, pharma, automotive, electronics, beverages, warehousing) running on identical framework code. Calibration experiments (60 runs, 10 seeds per template) confirm parametric controllability: observed KPIs fall within configured ranges across all templates. A controlled hallucination experiment (72 tool invocations, Qwen3-32B) demonstrates that ontology-constrained parameters eliminate tool-parameter fabrication (0% constrained vs. 43% unconstrained hallucination rate for the evaluated model, Fisher's exact test p < 10^-12); the 0% constrained rate is an architectural guarantee that holds for any model. The framework provides a reusable data layer for discrete manufacturing AI validation.

</details>


### [210] [LatentRouter: Can We Choose the Right Multimodal Model Before Seeing Its Answer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11301)
*Xueqi Cheng,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentRouter提出了一种基于反事实多模态效用预测的MLLM路由方法，通过提取多模态路由胶囊、模型能力token和潜在通信机制，预测各候选模型在查询上的表现，实现性能导向和性能-成本混合路由，在MMR-Bench和VL-RouterBench上超越多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM路由方法仅评估查询难度，未能充分利用不同MLLM在多模态能力上的异构优势（如OCR、图表理解、空间推理等），需要更精细的路由机制来匹配查询需求与模型能力。

Method: 1）提取学习到的多模态路由胶囊；2）为每个候选MLLM构建模型能力token；3）在路由状态与模型能力token之间进行潜在通信，估计各模型的选择效应；4）分布性结果头预测模型特异的反事实质量；5）利用有界胶囊纠正机制优化边界决策。

Result: 在MMR-Bench和VL-RouterBench上，LatentRouter优于固定模型、特征级路由和学习路由基线；在依赖视觉、布局敏感或推理导向的多模态任务组上增益最显著；潜在通信机制是性能提升的主要贡献者。

Conclusion: LatentRouter通过反事实效用预测有效实现了MLLM路由，支持动态候选池变化，兼顾性能与成本，展示了潜在通信在细粒度能力匹配中的关键作用。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have heterogeneous strengths across OCR, chart understanding, spatial reasoning, visual question answering, cost, and latency. Effective MLLM routing therefore requires more than estimating query difficulty: a router must match the multimodal requirements of the current image-question input with the capabilities of each candidate model. We propose LatentRouter, a router that formulates MLLM routing as counterfactual multimodal utility prediction. Given an image-question query, LatentRouter extracts learned multimodal routing capsules, represents each candidate MLLM with a model capability token, and performs latent communication between these states to estimate how each model would perform if selected. A distributional outcome head predicts model-specific counterfactual quality, while a bounded capsule correction refines close decisions without allowing residual signals to dominate the prediction. The resulting utility-based policy supports performance-oriented and performance-cost routing, and handles changing candidate pools through shared per-model scoring with availability masking. Experiments on MMR-Bench and VL-RouterBench show that LatentRouter outperforms fixed-model, feature-level, and learned-router baselines. Additional analyses show that the gains are strongest on multimodal task groups where model choice depends on visual, layout-sensitive, or reasoning-oriented requirements, and that latent communication is the main contributor to the improvement. The code is available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/LatentRouter.

</details>


### [211] [Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization: Utilizing Data Attribution for Effective Data Pruning in Low-Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11312)
*Danilo Brajovic,David A. Kreplin,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束数据价值最大化（CDVM）方法，解决了Shapley-based数据值在低数据量场景下不适用于数据剪枝的问题，通过将剪枝建模为约束优化，在保留少量数据时仍能取得鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley-based数据值在仅保留少量数据时，无法有效识别和剪除低价值数据，存在性能局限性。

Method: 提出约束数据价值最大化（CDVM）方法，将数据剪枝建模为约束优化问题，同时最大化总体影响力并惩罚过度的每测试样本贡献。

Result: 在OpenDataVal基准上，CDVM方法展现出强性能和具有竞争力的运行时间。

Conclusion: CDVM有效弥补了现有数据归因方法在低数据量场景下的不足，为数据剪枝提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Attributing model behavior to training data is an evolving research field. A common benchmark is data removal, which involves eliminating data instances with either low or high values, then assessing a model's performance trained on the modified dataset. Many existing studies leverage Shapley-based data values for this task. In this paper, we demonstrate that these data values are not optimally suited for pruning low-value data when only a limited amount of data remains. To address this limitation, we introduce the Constraint-Data-Value-Maximization (CDVM) approach, which effectively utilizes data attributions for pruning in low-data scenarios. By casting pruning as a constrained optimization that both maximizes total influence and penalizes excessive per-test contributions, CDVM delivers robust performance when only a small fraction of the data is retained. On the OpenDataVal benchmark, CDVM shows strong performance and competitive runtime.

</details>


### [212] [Rethinking Evaluation for LLM Hallucination Detection: A Desiderata, A New RAG-based Benchmark, New Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11330)
*Wenbo Chen,Veena Padmanabhan,Tootiya Giyahchi,Elaine Wong,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有幻觉检测基准（HDBs）在长上下文RAG场景和真实标签噪声方面存在两大缺陷，并构建了新的RAG基准TRIVIA+，该基准具有最长上下文样本和四种噪声标签方案。实验表明当前检测器在RAG基准上仍有很大提升空间，LLM-as-a-Judge基线表现竞争力强，标签噪声会降低检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉检测基准缺乏对RAG场景下长上下文样本的覆盖，且未提供真实标签噪声来测试检测器的鲁棒性，导致评估与实际应用存在差距。

Method: 建立了理想基准的属性需求框架，识别出两大缺陷后，通过严格人工标注构建了RAG基准TRIVIA+，并设计了四种噪声方案（包括样本依赖和样本独立两种类型）。

Result: TRIVIA+在上下文长度上超过现有所有基准；实验发现当前SOTA检测器在RAG基准上远未达到性能上限，LLM-as-a-Judge基线表现具有竞争力，标签噪声会显著降低检测性能。

Conclusion: 需要更多RAG场景下的幻觉检测研究，TRIVIA+为未来工作提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Hallucination, broadly referring to unfaithful, fabricated, or inconsistent content generated by LLMs, has wide-ranging implications. Therefore, a large body of effort has been devoted to detecting LLM hallucinations, as well as designing benchmark datasets for evaluating these detectors. In this work, we first establish a desiderata of properties for hallucination detection benchmarks (HDBs) to exhibit for effective evaluation. A critical look at existing HDBs through the lens of our desiderata reveals that none of them exhibits all the properties. We identify two largest gaps: (1) RAG-based grounded benchmarks with long context are severely lacking (partly because length impedes human annotation); and (2) Existing benchmarks do not make available realistic label noise for stress-testing detectors although real-world use-cases often grapple with label noise due to human or automated/weak annotation. To close these gaps, we build and open-source a new RAG-based HDB called T RIVIA+ that underwent a rigorous human annotation process. Notably, our benchmark exhibits all desirable properties including (1) T RIVIA+ contains samples with the longest context in the literature; and (2) we design and share four sets of noisy labels with different, both sample-dependent and sampleindependent, noise schemes. Finally, we perform experiments on RAG-based HDBs, including our T RIVIA+, using popular SOTA detectors that reveal new insights: (i) ample room remains for current detectors to reach the performance ceiling on RAG-based HDBs, (ii) the basic LLM-as-a-Judge baseline performs competitively, and (iii) label noise hinders detection performance. We expect that our findings, along with our proposed benchmark 1 , will motivate and foster needed research on hallucination detection for RAG-based tasks.

</details>


### [213] [CPEMH: An Agentic Framework for Prompt-Driven Behavior Evaluation and Assurance in Foundation-Model Systems for Mental Health Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11341)
*Giuliano Lorenzoni,Ivens Portugal,Paulo Alencar,Donald Cowan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CPEMH是一个用于心理健康筛查场景中评估基础模型提示驱动行为的智能体框架，通过模块化编排架构实现提示策略的自主设计、评估和选择，确保行为可追溯性、可复现性和鲁棒性。抑郁症筛查案例研究验证了其在临床敏感领域稳定和审计模型行为的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言系统在心理健康筛查等临床敏感场景中存在行为变异性问题，缺乏系统化的方法来自主设计、评估和选择提示策略，以控制跨上下文的行为变异性并确保行为保证。

Method: 提出CPEMH框架，采用模块化智能体设计，包含编排器、推理和评估三个智能体，自主执行提示策略的设计、评估和选择全生命周期管理，实现行为保证工程方法论。

Result: 在基于访谈转录本的自动抑郁症筛查案例研究中，验证了框架在临床敏感对话领域中稳定和审计基础模型行为的能力，特别是稳定性的优先保障优于架构复杂性。

Conclusion: 模块化编排在行为保证中起关键作用，应将稳定性置于架构复杂性之上，并将F1分数、偏差和鲁棒性作为核心验收标准纳入评估体系。

Abstract: This paper presents CPEMH, an agentic framework designed to evaluate prompt-driven behavior in foundation-model systems operating on transcript-based datasets for mental-health screening. CPEMH serves as an engineering methodology for behavioral assurance in large-scale language systems, introducing an orchestrated architecture that autonomously performs the design, evaluation, and selection of prompt strategies, enabling systematic control of behavioral variability across contexts. Its modular agentic design, combining orchestrator, inference, and evaluation agents, ensures traceability, reproducibility, and robustness throughout the prompting lifecycle. A case study on automated depression screening from interview transcripts demonstrates the framework's capacity to stabilize and audit foundation-model behavior in conversational and clinically sensitive domains. Lessons learned emphasize the role of modular orchestration in behavioral assurance, the prioritization of stability over architectural complexity, and the integration of F1, bias, and robustness as core acceptance criteria.

</details>


### [214] [CVEvolve: Autonomous Algorithm Discovery for Unstructured Scientific Data Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11359)
*Ming Du,Xiangyu Yin,Yanqi Luo,Dishant Beniwal,Songyuan Tang,Hemant Sharma,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CVEvolve，一个零代码自主智能体框架，通过多轮搜索、代码执行、评估实现和留出测试等工具，自动发现科学数据处理算法。在X射线荧光显微镜图像配准、布拉格峰检测和高能衍射显微镜图像分割三个任务上，CVEvolve发现的算法优于基线方法，验证了零代码、LLM驱动的算法开发对领域科学家的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据处理通常需要任务特定的算法或AI模型，这对不具备丰富计算或图像处理专业知识的领域科学家构成了障碍，尤其是在数据存在噪声、高动态范围、稀疏标注或仅松散定义的情况下。

Method: CVEvolve是一个自主智能体框架，提供零代码接口。它结合多轮搜索策略与代码执行、评估实现、历史管理、留出测试以及可选的科学数据和视觉输出检查工具。搜索过程交替进行发现和改进动作，并使用谱系感知的随机候选采样来平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在X射线荧光显微镜图像配准、布拉格峰检测和高能衍射显微镜图像分割三个任务上，CVEvolve发现的算法优于基线方法。留出测试跟踪有助于识别泛化能力更好的候选算法，避免后期过度优化的替代方案。

Conclusion: 零代码、自主LLM驱动的算法开发能够帮助领域科学家将非结构化的科学图像数据转化为实用算法，推动下游科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific data processing often requires task-specific algorithms or AI models, creating a barrier for domain scientists who need to analyze their data but may not have extensive computing or image-processing expertise. This barrier is especially pronounced when data are noisy, have a high dynamic range, are sparsely labeled, or are only loosely specified. We introduce CVEvolve, an autonomous agentic harness with a zero-code interface for scientific data-processing algorithm discovery. CVEvolve combines a multi-round search strategy with tools for code execution, evaluation implementation, history management, holdout testing, and optional inspection of scientific data and visual outputs. The search alternates between discovery and improvement actions, and uses lineage-aware stochastic candidate sampling to balance exploration and exploitation. We demonstrate CVEvolve on x-ray fluorescence microscopy image registration, Bragg peak detection, and high-energy diffraction microscopy image segmentation. Across these tasks, CVEvolve discovers algorithms that improve over baseline methods, while holdout test tracking helps identify candidates that generalize better than later over-optimized alternatives. These results show that zero-code, autonomous LLM-powered algorithm development can help domain scientists turn unstructured scientific image data into practical algorithms and downstream scientific discoveries.

</details>


### [215] [Causal Bias Detection in Generative Artifical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11365)
*Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了生成式AI中的因果公平性问题，提出了统一标准ML与生成式AI的因果公平理论框架，推导了新的因果分解方法以量化不同因果路径和机制替换带来的公平影响，并建立了识别条件与高效估计量，通过大语言模型中的种族与性别偏见分析验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果公平研究主要针对标准机器学习设定（单个预测机制），但生成式AI能够从任意条件分布中采样，隐式构建所有因果机制，这与标准ML存在根本差异，亟需新的因果公平方法论。

Method: 1. 形式化生成式AI中的因果公平问题，并与标准ML设定统一于一个共同的理论框架；2. 推导新的因果分解结果，实现沿不同因果路径以及真实世界机制被生成模型机制替换两个维度对公平影响的精细量化；3. 建立识别条件并引入高效估计量。

Result: 通过分析大语言模型在不同数据集上的种族和性别偏见，验证了所提方法论的有效性。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的因果公平问题需要新的理论和方法，本文提出的框架成功扩展了因果公平的范围，能够更精细地量化生成模型中的公平影响。

Abstract: Automated systems built on artificial intelligence (AI) are increasingly deployed across high-stakes domains, raising critical concerns about fairness and the perpetuation of demographic disparities that exist in the world. In this context, causal inference provides a principled framework for reasoning about fairness, as it links observed disparities to underlying mechanisms and aligns naturally with human intuition and legal notions of discrimination. Prior work on causal fairness primarily focuses on the standard machine learning setting, where a decision-maker constructs a single predictive mechanism $f_{\widehat Y}$ for an outcome variable $Y$, while inheriting the causal mechanisms of all other covariates from the real world. The generative AI setting, however, is markedly more complex: generative models can sample from arbitrary conditionals over any set of variables, implicitly constructing their own beliefs about all causal mechanisms rather than learning a single predictive function. This fundamental difference requires new developments in causal fairness methodology. We formalize the problem of causal fairness in generative AI and unify it with the standard ML setting under a common theoretical framework. We then derive new causal decomposition results that enable granular quantification of fairness impacts along both (a) different causal pathways and (b) the replacement of real-world mechanisms by the generative model's mechanisms. We establish identification conditions and introduce efficient estimators for causal quantities of interest, and demonstrate the value of our methodology by analyzing race and gender bias in large language models across different datasets.

</details>


### [216] [Causal Algorithmic Recourse: Foundations and Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11373)
*Drago Plecko,Collin Wang,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个因果框架来建模算法追索（algorithmic recourse）问题，将追索视为个体在干预前后潜在变量部分稳定或重新采样下的重复决策过程。通过引入追索后稳定性条件，开发了基于copula的算法和分布自由算法来推断追索效应，并在真实和半合成数据集上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法将追索结果视为固定个体的反事实，忽略了现实世界中追索涉及同一个体在不同潜在条件下的重复决策过程。本文旨在建立一个更符合实际的因果框架来建模这种动态过程。

Method: 1) 提出一个因果框架建模追索为干预前后结果的动态过程，允许潜在变量部分稳定或重新采样；2) 引入追索后稳定性条件(post-recourse stability conditions)，使得仅从观测数据即可推理追索效应；3) 开发基于copula的算法来推断追索效应；4) 针对存在成对追索数据的情况，提出copula参数推断和拟合优度检验方法；5) 当copula模型被拒绝时，提供一种分布自由算法直接从追索数据学习追索效应。

Result: 在真实和半合成数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性，证明所提出的框架能够更准确地建模和推断现实中的追索效应。

Conclusion: 算法追索应被视为一个动态过程而非静态反事实，本文的因果框架和算法为理解和实现更可靠的追索提供了理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: The trustworthiness of AI decision-making systems is increasingly important. A key feature of such systems is the ability to provide recommendations for how an individual may reverse a negative decision, a problem known as algorithmic recourse.
